- **compose-retire-boundary-marker**: Remove a stray second copy of the word boundary (`#`) or morpheme boundary (`+`) marker from the first phoneme set. - **compose-rewrite-lexeme-form**: Rewrites the lexeme forms of existing entries, many in one Draft, preserving each form's identity, references, conditions and other writing-system alternatives. Motif refuses punctuation for a letter, mark or modifier, because no writing-system setting can make one so. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-set-character-class.md) · ★1)
- **compose-set-entry-attribution**: Records where lexical entries came from, in the Bibliography of each entry, as the Attribution tag `&lt;source&gt;@&lt;revision&gt; &lt;licence&gt;`, such as `lang-sme@9ba7be7 GPL-3.0`. The composer refuses a missing or wrong-type target and an `expectedDoNotParse` value that does not match the saved project. - **compose-set-no-default-compounding**: The parser joins any two stems into a word by default, even when the project has no compound rules, unless the project's parser settings say otherwise. - **compose-set-not-on-clitics**: The parser runs the project's phonological rules on a word before any clitic attaches to it, unless the project's parser settings say otherwise.
